If not done no new events will be emitted for this key.\\n46: // If an event handling is marked as failed the configured error handler is called\\n47: // (WithErrorHandler). The default error handler will requeue the event (by its key) for\\n48: // later retried processing. The requeueing is rate limited and can be configured with\\n49: // WithRateLimiter option to Events().\\n50: //\\n51: // The resource is lazy, e.g. it will not start the informer until a call\\n52: // has been made to Events() or Store().\\n53: type Resource[T k8sRuntime.Object] interface {\\n54: \\t// Resource can be observed either via Observe() or via Events(). The observable\\n55: \\t// is implemented in terms of Events() and same semantics apply.\\n56: \\tstream.Observable[Event[T]]\\n57: \\n58: \\t// Events returns a channel of events. Each event must be marked as handled\\n59: \\t// with a call to Done() which marks the key processed. No new events for this key\\n60: \\t// will be emitted before Done() is called.\\n61: \\t//\\n62: \\t// A missing Done() will lead to an eventual panic (via finalizer on Event[T]).\\n63: \\t// Panic on this situation is needed as otherwise no new events would be emitted\\n64: \\t// and thus this needs to be enforced.\\n65: \\t//\\n66: \\t// A stream of Upsert events are emitted first to replay the current state of the\\n67: \\t// store after which incremental upserts and deletes follow until the underlying\\n68: \\t// store is synchronized after which a Sync event is emitted and further incremental\\n69: \\t// updates:\\n70: \\t//\\n71: \\t//\\t(start observing), Upsert, Upsert, Upsert, (done replaying store contents), Upsert, Upsert,\\n72: \\t//\\t  (store synchronized with API server), Sync, Upsert, Delete, Upsert, ...\\n73: \\t//\\n74: \\t// The emitting of the Sync event does not depend on whether or not Upsert events have\\n75: \\t// all been marked Done() without an error. The sync event solely signals that the underlying\\n76: \\t// store has synchronized and that Upsert events for objects in a synchronized store have been\\n77: \\t// sent to the observer.\\n78: \\t//\\n79: \\t// When Done() is called with non-nil error the error handler is invoked, which\\n80: \\t// can ignore, requeue the event (by key) or close the channel. The default error handler\\n81: \\t// will requeue.\\n82: \\t//\\n83: \\t// If an Upsert is retried and the object has been deleted, a Delete event will be emitted instead.\\n84: \\t// Conversely if a Delete event is retried and the object has been recreated with the same key,\\n85: \\t// an Upsert will be emitted instead.\\n86: \\t//\\n87: \\t// If an objects is created and immediately deleted, then a slow observer may not observe this at\\n88: \\t// all. In all cases a Delete event is only emitted if the observer has seen an Upsert. Whether or\\n89: \\t// not it had been successfully handled (via Done(nil)) does not affect this property.\\n90: \\tEvents(ctx context.Context, opts ...EventsOpt) <-chan Event[T]\\n91: \\n92: \\t// Store retrieves the read-only store for the resource. Blocks until\\n93: \\t// the store has been synchronized or the context cancelled.\\n94: \\t// Returns a non-nil error if context is cancelled or the resource\\n95: \\t// has been stopped before store has synchronized.\\n96: \\tStore(context.Context) (Store[T], error)\\n97: }\\n98: \\n99: // New creates a new Resource[T]. Use with hive.Provide:\\n100: //\\n101: //\\tvar exampleCell = hive.Module(\\n102: //\\t\\t\\\"example\\\",\\n103: //\\t \\tcell.Provide(\\n104: //\\t\\t \\t// Provide `Resource[*slim_corev1.Pod]` to //\\t\\t\\t\\t\\tc.Slim().CoreV1().Pods(\\\"\\\"),\\n108: //\\t\\t\\t\\t)\\n109: //\\t\\t \\t\\treturn resource.New(lc, lw)\\n110: //\\t\\t \\t}\\n111: //\\t\\t}),\\n112: //\\t\\t...\\n113: //\\t)\\n114: //\\n115: //\\tfunc usePods(pods resource.Resource[*slim_corev1.Pod]) {\\n116: //\\t\\tgo func() {\\n117: //\\t\\t\\tfor ev := range podEvents {\\n118: //\\t\\t   \\t\\tonPodEvent(ev)\\n119: //\\t\\t   \\t}\\n120: //\\t\\t}\\n121: //\\t\\treturn e\\n122: //\\t}\\n123: //\\tfunc onPodEvent(event resource.Event[*slim_core.Pod]) {\\n124: //\\t\\tswitch event.Kind {\\n125: //\\t\\tcase resource.Sync:\\n126: //\\t\\t\\t// Pods have now been synced and the set of Upsert events\\n127: //\\t\\t\\t// received thus far forms a coherent snapshot.\\n128: //\\n129: //\\t\\t\\t// Must always call event.Done(error) to mark the event as processed.\\n130: //\\t\\t\\tevent.Done(nil)\\n131: //\\t\\tcase resource.Upsert:\\n132: //\\t\\t\\tevent.Done(onPodUpsert(event.Object))\\n133: //\\t\\tcase resource.Delete:\\n134: //\\t\\t\\tevent.Done(onPodDelete(event.Object))\\n135: //\\t\\t}\\n136: //\\t}\\n137: //\\n138: // See also pkg/k8s/resource/example/main.go for a runnable example.\\n139: func New[T k8sRuntime.Object](lc cell.Lifecycle, lw cache.ListerWatcher, opts ...ResourceOption) Resource[T] {\\n140: \\tr := The requeueing is rate limited and can be configured with\\n49: // WithRateLimiter option to Events().\\n50: //\\n51: // The resource is lazy, e.g. it will not start the informer until a call\\n52: // has been made to Events() or Store().\\n53: type Resource[T k8sRuntime.Object] interface {\\n54: \\t// Resource can be observed either via Observe() or via Events(). The sync event solely signals that the underlying\\n76: \\t// store has synchronized and that Upsert events for objects in a synchronized store have been\\n77: \\t// sent to the observer.\\n78: \\t//\\n79: \\t// When Done() is called with non-nil error the error handler is invoked, which\\n80: \\t// can ignore, requeue the event (by key) or close the channel. The default error handler\\n81: \\t// will requeue.\\n82: \\t//\\n83: \\t// If an Upsert is retried and the object has been deleted, a Delete event will be emitted instead.\\n84: \\t// Conversely if a Delete event is retried and the object has been recreated with the same key,\\n85: \\t// an Upsert will be emitted instead.\\n86: \\t//\\n87: \\t// If an objects is created and immediately deleted, then a slow observer may not observe this at\\n88: \\t// all. In all cases a Delete event is only emitted if the observer has seen an Upsert. Whether or\\n89: \\t// not it had been successfully handled (via Done(nil)) does not affect this property.\\n90: \\tEvents(ctx context.Context, opts ...EventsOpt) <-chan Event[T]\\n91: \\n92: \\t// Store retrieves the read-only store for the resource. Blocks until\\n93: \\t// the store has been synchronized or the context cancelled.\\n94: \\t// Returns a non-nil error if context is cancelled or the resource\\n95: \\t// has been stopped before store has synchronized.\\n96: \\tStore(context.Context) (Store[T], error)\\n97: }\\n98: \\n99: // New creates a new Resource[T]. events or a read-only store.\n35: //\n36: // Observing of the events can be done from a constructor as subscriber\n37: // registration is non-blocking.\n38: //\n39: // Store() however should only be The sync event solely signals that the underlying\n76: \t// store has synchronized and that Upsert events for objects in a synchronized store have been\n77: \t// sent to the observer.\n78: \t//\n79: \t// When Done() is called with non-nil error the error handler is invoked, which\n80: \t// can ignore, requeue the event (by key) or close the channel. := NewPolicyQuotaController(log, mockRes, 100)\n\n\t// Create policies\n\tpolicies := make([]*cilium_v2.CiliumNetworkPolicy, 0, 50)\n\tfor i := 0; i < 50; i++ {\n\t\tpolicies = append(policies, &cilium_v2.CiliumNetworkPolicy{\n\t\t\tObjectMeta: metav1.ObjectMeta{\n\t\t\t\tName:      \"policy-\" + string(rune(i)),\n\t\t\t\tNamespace: \"default\",\n\t\t\t},\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\t// Concurrent {\n\t\t\tcontroller.onPolicyDelete(p)\n\t\t\tdone <- struct{}{}\n\t\t}(policy)\n\t}\n\n\tfor range policies {\n\t\t<-done\n\t}\n\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, controller.GetPolicyCount(\"default\"))\n}\n\nfunc TestNewPolicyQuotaController(t *testing.T) {\n\tlog := logrus.NewEntry(logrus.New())\n\tmockRes := newMockResource()\n\n\tt.Run(\"initialization\", Controller (controller.go)\nMain controller that watches and enforces policy quotas.\n\n#### Constructor\n```go\ncontroller := NewPolicyQuotaController(\n    log,                              // logrus.FieldLogger\n    ciliumNetworkPolicies,            // resource.Resource[*CiliumNetworkPolicy]\n    5,                                // max policies per namespace\n)\n```\n\n#### Run the Controller\n```go\n// Start watching policies (blocks until context cancelled)\nif err := controller.Run(ctx); err != nil {\n    // Handle error\n}\n```\n\n#### Check Quota Before Creating Policy\n```go\nif err := controller.CheckQuota(\"default\"); err != nil {\n    // Quota exceeded - reject policy creation\n    log.Warnf(\"Cannot create policy: %v\", err)\n    return err\n}\n```\n\n#### Query Current Counts\n```go\n// Get count for specific namespace\ncount := controller.GetPolicyCount(\"default\")\n\n// Get all namespace counts\ncounts := controller.GetNamespacePolicyCounts()\n// Returns: map[string]int{\"default\": 2, \"kube-system\": 1}\n```\n\n### 3. Test Suite (controller_test.go)\nComprehensive tests covering all functionality.\n\nRun tests:\n```bash\ngo test ./pkg/policy/quota/... -v\n```\n\n## Key Design Features\n\n### Thread Safety\n- All public methods use RWMutex\n- Safe for concurrent access\n- Multiple readers can query simultaneously\n- Writer operations properly synchronized\n\n### Event Handling\n- Watches CiliumNetworkPolicy resources\n- Processes Upsert, Delete, and Sync events\n- Properly marks each event as Done()\n- Supports context cancellation\n\n### Quota Enforcement\n- Per-namespace counting\n- Returns error when limit reached\n- Detailed error messages with metrics\n- Current count and max quota included\n\n## Error Messages\n\nQuota Exceeded Error:\n```\npolicy quota exceeded in namespace \"default\": current=5, max=5\n```\n\n## Metrics Included in Error\n\nWhen quota is exceeded, log includes:\n- `logfields.K8sNamespace`: target namespace\n- `current_count`: current number of policies\n- `max_per_namespace`: configured limit\n\n## Integration Points\n\n### Dependencies\n- `logrus.FieldLogger`: Structured logging\n- `resource.Resource[T]`: Kubernetes resource watching\n- `cilium_v2.CiliumNetworkPolicy`: CNP resource type\n\n### Used By\n- Policy admission webhooks (to validate quota)\n- Policy controllers (to track additions/deletions)\n- Management APIs (to query current usage)\n\n## Data Flow\n\n```\nK8s API Server\n    ↓\nresource.Resource (watches CNPs)\n    ↓\nPolicyQuotaController.Run()\n    ├─ Upsert Event → onPolicyUpsert() → policyCount++\n    ├─ Delete Event → onPolicyDelete() → policyCount--\n    └─ Sync Event → Log sync completion\n    ↓\nCheckQuota() ← External API\n    ↓\nReturns error if exceeded\n```\n\n## Testing Patterns\n\n### Mock Resource for Tests\n```go\nmockRes := &mockResource{\n    eventsChan: make(chan resource.Event[*cilium_v2.CiliumNetworkPolicy], 100),\n}\ncontroller := NewPolicyQuotaController(log, mockRes, 5)\n```\n\n### Sending Test Events\n```go\nevent := resource.Event[*cilium_v2.CiliumNetworkPolicy]{\n    Kind:   resource.Upsert,\n    Object: policy,\n    Key:    resource.Key{Name: \"policy-1\", Namespace: \"default\"},\n    Done:   func(err error) {},\n}\nmockRes.eventsChan <- event\n```\n\n## Common Scenarios\n\n### Scenario 1: Prevent Policy Sprawl\n```go\n// Before creating policy in webhook\nif err := controller.CheckQuota(policy.Namespace); err != nil {\n    return admission.Denied(err.Error())\n}\n// Allow creation\nreturn admission.Allowed(\"quota OK\")\n```\n\n### Scenario 2: Monitor Usage\n```go\n// Periodically log current usage\nticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)\nfor range ticker.C {\n    counts := controller.GetNamespacePolicyCounts()\n    log.Infof(\"Current policy counts: %v\", counts)\n}\n```\n\n### Scenario 3: Handle Namespace Cleanup\n```go\n// When namespace is deleted, count automatically cleaned up\n// (no entries in map for empty namespaces)\ncontroller.onPolicyDelete(policy)  // Decrements count\n// If count reaches 0, namespace is removed from map\n```\n\n## Performance Characteristics\n\n- **Memory**: O(n) where n = number of namespaces with policies\n- **CheckQuota**: O(1) read operation\n- **Policy Add/Delete**: O(1) map operations\n- **Query All**: O(n) where n = number of namespaces\n- **Thread Safety**: RWMutex (minimal lock contention for reads)\n\n## Debugging\n\n### Enable Debug Logging\n```go\n// Controller uses logrus with \"policy-quota\" subsystem\n// Configure logrus level to debug\nlogrus.SetLevel(logrus.DebugLevel)\n```\n\n### Monitor Events\n```\nDEBUG Policy upserted: namespace=default, policy_count=3, max_per_namespace=5\nDEBUG Policy deleted: namespace=default, policy_count=2\nWARN Policy quota exceeded: namespace=default, current_count=5, max_per_namespace=5\n```\nEOF\ncat
